2026-09-13T00:35:24.1502715Z     
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1503064Z     - Detailed info on failing `assert statements <https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/how-to/assert.html>`_ (no need to remember ``self.assert*`` names)
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1503391Z     
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1503719Z     - `Auto-discovery
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1504056Z       <https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/explanation/goodpractices.html#python-test-discovery>`_
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1504389Z       of test modules and functions
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1504714Z     
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1505047Z     - `Modular fixtures <https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/explanation/fixtures.html>`_ for
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1505383Z       managing small or parametrized long-lived test resources
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1505705Z     
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1506038Z     - Can run `unittest <https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/how-to/unittest.html>`_ (or trial)
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1506370Z       test suites out of the box
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1506694Z     
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1507024Z     - Python 3.10+ or PyPy3
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1507346Z     
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1507694Z     - Rich plugin architecture, with over 1300+ `external plugins <https://docs.pytest.org/en/latest/reference/plugin_list.html>`_ and thriving community
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1508020Z     
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1508344Z     
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1508669Z     Documentation
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1509030Z     -------------
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1509410Z     
2026-09-13T00:35:24.1509974Z     For full documentation, including installation, tutorials and PDF documents, please see https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/.
